# Gemstone Mixture
Aliases: Mixture
Categories: Rare items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Forged items
[Gemstone Mixture]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Heart of the Mountain IV (4) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
Tooltip Text: A collection of finely treated Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) held together by the sturdiest of sticky substances.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600x Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600x Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600x Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Sludge Juice]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GEMSTONE_MIXTURE
Tooltip: A collection of finely treated Gemstones held together by the sturdiest of sticky substances.; RARE
The Gemstone Mixture is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
The Gemstone Mixture can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]]. It requires 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]], 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]], 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Amethyst Gemstone]], 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]], 320 (5 stacks) [[Sludge Juice]] and 4 hours to forge. If Cole is in office with his Molten Forge perk, this time is reduced to 3 hours. With the Heart of the Mountain perk Quick Forge, this time is reduced to 2 hours and 28 minutes. Both of these combined reduce the forge time to 2 hours and 6 minutes.
It is also a rare drop (0.23%) from the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
### Forging Tree
- [[Gemstone Mixture]] 1 Gemstone Mixture
  - 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Jade Gemstone]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Jade Gemstone]]
  - 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amber Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Amber Gemstone]]
  - 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Amethyst Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amethyst Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Amethyst Gemstone]]
  - 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Sapphire Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Sapphire Gemstone]]
  - 320 [[Sludge Juice]]

## Trivia
- Gemstone Mixture doesn't require [[Gemstone|Ruby]], [[Gemstone|Topaz]], or [[Gemstone|Jasper]] out of the 7 types of [[Crystal Hollows]] [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], but it does require the other 4.
- Gemstone Mixture used to have the same texture as the [[Lantern]] in the first few days of the alpha testing.

# General's Medallion
Aliases: Medallion
Categories: Rare items, Accessories, Dungeons, Catacombs
[General's Medallion]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Once worn proudly, now covered in filth and soot.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Zodd]]
Buy: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GENERAL_MEDALLION
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Increases the base stats of your dungeon items by 1% while in The Catacombs for each digit on your secret count. (Max 5%); Counter: 0; Bonus: +0%; Once worn proudly, now covered in filth and soot.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE DUNGEON ACCESSORY
The General's Medallion is a [[Rarity|RARE]] Dungeon [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
The General's Medallion is purchasable from [[Zodd]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] or higher.
## Upgrading
The General's Medallion tracks how many [[Dungeon Secrets]] the Player found and increases the base stats of dungeon items by 1% while in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] for each digit in their Secret Count.
|Secrets|Stat Increase|
|-|-|
|0||
|1|+1%|
|10|+2%|
|100|+3%|
|1000|+4%|
|10000|+5%|
## Notes
Unlike some other counters such as the [[Runebook]], the General's Medallion will count secrets even if the player does not yet have it in their Inventory or [[Accessory Bag]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

# Geometric Oddity
Aliases: Geometric
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Craftable items
[Geometric Oddity]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Geometric
Applied To: [[Froggles]] [[Silver Froggles]] [[Golden Froggles]] [[Diamond Froggles]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 32x [[Ink Sac]] 20x [[Rusty Coin]] 8x [[Frogcoin]] 1x [[EroC enotS egrofeR]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GEOMETRIC_ODDITY
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Geometric reforge when combined with Froggles, Silver Froggles, Golden Froggles, and Diamond Froggles.; A man, who knows not where he is nor where he has been, reaches into his pocket.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Fishing Speed  Trophy Chance; Geometric Bonus Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 15.; RARE REFORGE STONE
A Geometric Oddity is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Geometric]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Froggles]], [[Silver Froggles]], [[Golden Froggles]], or [[Diamond Froggles]]. It grants [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Trophy Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] 8x [[Frogcoin]] 16x [[Rusty Coin]] 32x [[Ink Sac]] 64x [[Treasure Bait]]|Recipe: [[Ink Sac]] x32 + [[Rusty Coin]] x16 + [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] + [[Frogcoin]] x8 + [[Treasure Bait]] x64 → Geometric Oddity|
## Usage
### Reforging
Geometric Oddity can be combined with [[Froggles]], [[Silver Froggles]], [[Golden Froggles]], or [[Diamond Froggles]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Geometric]] reforge, to grant [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Trophy Chance]], which scale with the Helmet's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XV (15)]]. A Helmet that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Geometric]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Geometric Bonus, which gives the player a 5% (1 in 20) chance to obtain a copy of a caught [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophy]], when fishing in a [[Wormhole]] .
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+0.5 TPC ([[Trophy Chance]]) +0.5 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+1 TPC ([[Trophy Chance]]) +1 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+1.5 TPC ([[Trophy Chance]]) +1.5 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+2 TPC ([[Trophy Chance]]) +2 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+3 TPC ([[Trophy Chance]]) +3 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+4 TPC ([[Trophy Chance]]) +4 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+5 TPC ([[Trophy Chance]]) +5 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Geometric Bonus; Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.|Geometric Bonus; Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.|Geometric Bonus; Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.|Geometric Bonus; Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.|Geometric Bonus; Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.|Geometric Bonus; Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.|Geometric Bonus; Gain a +5% chance to catch a bonus Trophy while fishing in a Wormhole.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The following message is sent in chat upon activating the Geometric bonus:
GEOMETRIC! Your Geometric fished you up a bonus Trophy!
- The Geometric bonus of doubling [[Trophy Frogs]] does not multiply [[Pity and RNG Meters|Pity]], nor the total catches caught for [[Researcher Ribery]].

Tooltip: Aquatic Fortune I Grants +0.5  Magic Find against  Aquatic mobs.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R89)
The Giant Water Bug Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Aquatic Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
A Giant Water Bug Shard can be obtained from [[Fishing]] in the [[Hotspot Haven]]. This area contains a [[Fishing Hotspots|Fishing Hotspot]], which replaces every [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]] with [[Giant Water Bug Shard]] 1 Giant Water Bug Shard and every [[Treasure|OUTSTANDING CATCH]] with [[Giant Water Bug Shard]] 4–8 Giant Water Bug Shards, both affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
### Huntraps
A Giant Water Bug Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Hotspot Haven]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Giant Water Bug Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Giant Water Bug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Aquatic Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Aquatic Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] against [[Aquatic]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Giant Water Bug Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Giant Water Bug Shard has the following description:
- Giant Water Bugs are able to breathe underwater through the use of a snorkel-like appendage, and boast a painful bite.

Up to 8 Gift of Learning by reaching Gift Milestone XXV at [[Generow]].
A Gift of Learning can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from the [[Raffle of the Century]] or [[Gift Bag of the Century]].

Players can use a Gift of Learning to grant themselves +10,000 [[Skills|Skill]] XP to one of 8 different skills:
- [[Farming]]
- [[Mining]]
- [[Combat]]
- [[Foraging]]
- [[Fishing]]
- [[Enchanting]]
- [[Alchemy]]
- [[Hunting]]

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Grants +15☯ Mining Wisdom on Jerry's Workshop.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
Glacial Artifact is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants the [[Player]] +15 [[Mining Wisdom]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Glacial Ring]] and is the final Accessory in the "Glacial" line.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ice VIII (8) ([[Ice]])|1x [[Glacial Ring]] 8x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 40x [[Glacial Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Glacial Fragment]] x10 ×4 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x2 ×4 + [[Glacial Ring]] → Glacial Artifact|
## Usage
Accessory:
Glacial Artifact
[Effects]
[Grants +15 Mining Wisdom while on Jerry's Workshop.]
### Salvaging
Glacial Artifact can be salvaged for 15 [[Ice Essence]].
## Tips
- It is generally not recommended to salvage this item, as it gives the same amount of [[Ice Essence]] as the [[Glacial Talisman]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 30, 2022|Added Glacial Artifact.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Glacial Artifact can now be salved for 15 [[Ice Essence]].|

The Glacite Walker Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Essence of Ice]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Glacite Walker Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Glacite Walker]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Glacite Walker Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Great Ice Wall]].
### Fusion
A Glacite Walker Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Glacite Walker Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Essence of Ice]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Essence of Ice ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% more [[Ice Essence]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Glacite Walker Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Glowing Mush Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Brewing, Potions, Fishing
[Glowing Mush Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Moby]]
Buy: 64x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Glowing Mushroom]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MUSHED_MUSHROOM_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain +30 Fishing Speed if you're not getting it from the Mushed Glowy Tonic.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); RARE
Glowing Mush Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Purchase
A Glowing Mush Mixin can be purchased from [[Moby]] for 64 (1 stack) [[Chum Bucket|Full Chum Bucket]] and 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Glowing Mushroom]], worth at [[Bazaar]].
## Usage
### Brewing
A Glowing Mush Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +30 [[Fishing Speed]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Glowing Mush Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Bugs
- The effect of the Glowing Mush Mixin stays active even if the [[Mushed Glowy Tonic Potion|Mushed Glowy Tonic]] effect is disabled in the Toggle Potion Effects menu (which is normally accessible from the [[Booster Cookie]] menu).
  - However, since the mixin only grants its bonus if a [[Mushed Glowy Tonic Potion|Mushed Glowy Tonic]] Potion is not already active, this can be worked around by consuming or being splashed with one, and then disabling the effect will work as expected.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Added Glowing Mush Mixin.|

Goblin Egg is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] or [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblins]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]] or from chests in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv23]|[[Fireslinger]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[Lv23]|[[Goblin]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv89]|[[Creeperlobber]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.6%|
|[Lv65]|[[Goblin Flamethrower]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.6%|
|[Lv96]|[[Murderlover]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.6%|
|[Lv23]|[[Knifethrower]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
### Treasure Chests
Goblin Eggs can be dropped from chests in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. The chests can either be found in naturally spawning structures like the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] and by [[King Yolkar]] or can be spawned by the player while mining [[Hard Stone]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]].
### [[Frozen Corpses]]
Goblin Eggs can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].

|Lapis|[[Goblin Egg]] 1 Goblin Egg|4.78% (1 in 20.94)|

|Icon|Name|Rarity|Obtained via|Usage|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Green Goblin Egg]]|[[Green Goblin Egg]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Treasure Chests ([[Crystal Hollows]]) in the; [[Goblin Holdout]]|[[Pesto Goblin Omelette]]|
|[[Blue Goblin Egg]]|[[Blue Goblin Egg]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Treasure Chests ([[Crystal Hollows]]) in the; [[Goblin Holdout]]|[[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]|
|[[Red Goblin Egg]]|[[Red Goblin Egg]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Treasure Chests ([[Crystal Hollows]]) in the; [[Goblin Holdout]]|[[Spicy Goblin Omelette]]|
|[[Yellow Goblin Egg]]|[[Yellow Goblin Egg]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Treasure Chests ([[Crystal Hollows]]) in the; [[Goblin Holdout]]|[[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]]|
## Usage
### Spawning Golden Goblins
Throwing a Goblin Egg in [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] or [[Glacite Tunnels]] has a 2% (1 in 50 eggs) chance to spawn a [[Golden Goblin]], with a base 3.33% chance to be replaced with [[Diamond Goblin]].
### Fuel
The Goblin Egg can be used as a [[Machine Fuel]], adding 2,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]].
### Forging
#### [[Goblin Omelette]]
- 1 [[Goblin Omelette]]
  - [[Goblin Egg]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) Goblin Egg
### Amber Crystal
A [[Goblin Egg]] Goblin Egg of any type can be used to obtain [[Potions|King's Scent]] [[Potions|Potion Effect]] from [[King Yolkar]], which is used to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]].
## Trivia
- The first time spawning a [[Golden Goblin]] from throwing an egg will grant the [[Achievements|"The Next Generation" Achievement]].
- Throwing these contributes to the "Egg Thrower I" Hypixel achievement.
- The lore is a reference to the saying "which came first, the chicken or the egg?"

# Goblin Omelette

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_OMELETTE
Tooltip: Drill Part; Grants a 25% chance to not consume Fuel when mining a block.; Put this item in the Upgrade Module slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; May cause diseases; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 3.; RARE
The Goblin Omelette is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]] upgrade module that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It grants a 25% chance to not consume [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mining a block.
## Obtaining
The Goblin Omelette can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It requires 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Goblin Egg|Goblin Eggs]] and 18 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Goblin Omelette]] 1 Goblin Omelette
  - 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
## Usage
The Goblin Omelette can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to grant it a 25% chance to not consume Fuel when mining. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### [[The Forge|Forging]] Recipe
|Slot|Name|Forging ([[The Forge]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|
|[[Pesto Goblin Omelette]]|Pesto Goblin Omelette|1 [[Pesto Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Green Goblin Egg]] 1 Flawless Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 80 (1 stack plus 16) Fine Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 6,400 (100 stacks) Flawed Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 512,000 (8,000 stacks) Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Goblin Omelette]] 1 Goblin Omelette 96 [[Goblin Egg]]|
|[[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]]|Sunny Side Goblin Omelette|1 [[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Yellow Goblin Egg]] 1 Flawless Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 80 Fine Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 6,400 Flawed Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 512,000 Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Goblin Omelette]] 1 Goblin Omelette 96 [[Goblin Egg]]|
|[[Spicy Goblin Omelette]]|Spicy Goblin Omelette|1 [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Red Goblin Egg]] 1 Flawless Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 80 Fine Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 6,400 Flawed Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 512,000 Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Goblin Omelette]] 1 Goblin Omelette 96 [[Goblin Egg]]|
|[[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]|Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette|1 [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Blue Goblin Egg]] 1 Flawless Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 80 Fine Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 6,400 Flawed Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 512,000 Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Goblin Omelette]] 1 Goblin Omelette 96 [[Goblin Egg]]|
## Trivia
- The Goblin Omelette was the first released Drill Upgrade.

The Gold Macaw Parrot Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Gold Macaw Parrot Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on October 20, 2021. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 5,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Gold Macaw Parrot Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Parrot Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

Tooltip: Echo of Buckets I Your &quot;Bucket&quot; Attributes are +2%; stronger.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R86)
The Goldolot Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Echo of Buckets]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Goldolot Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Goldolot]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] or a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Huntraps
A Goldolot Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Spring Depths]] (Underwater), [[Spring Shallows]] (Underwater), or [[Torrhus Springs]] (On Land).
### Fusion
A Goldolot Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Goldolot Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Buckets]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Echo of Buckets ([[Attributes]]); Your "Bucket" Attributes are +2%–20% stronger.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Goldolot Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Goldolot Shard has the following description:
- Its splendid coloring, though magnificent, makes it an easy target.

Golem Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor]] set that is unlocked at [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingot VI (6)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
Golem Armor requires 240 (3 stacks plus 48) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] to craft a full set.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot VI (6) ([[Iron Ingot]])|50x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x10 ×8 → [[Golem Armor]]|
|Iron Ingot VI (6) ([[Iron Ingot]])|80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x10 ×8 → [[Golem Armor]]|
|Iron Ingot VI (6) ([[Iron Ingot]])|70x (1 stack plus 6 (70 in total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x10 ×7 → [[Golem Armor]]|
|Iron Ingot VI (6) ([[Iron Ingot]])|40x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x10 ×6 → [[Golem Armor]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Golem Armor Helmet ([[Golem Armor]]); [[Health]]: +45 [[Defense]]: +45|Golem Armor Chestplate ([[Golem Armor]]); [[Health]]: +65 [[Defense]]: +90|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Golem Armor Leggings ([[Golem Armor]]); [[Health]]: +55 [[Defense]]: +75|Golem Armor Boots ([[Golem Armor]]); [[Health]]: +40 [[Defense]]: +40|
[Total]
[Golem Armor Health: +205 Defense: +250]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Absorption; Grants the wearer Absorption III (Absorption Potion) for 20 seconds when they kill an enemy.]
## Trivia
- The Golem Armor's ability is another reference to the Hypixel mini game Mega Walls. In Mega Walls, the mighty Golem class has a passive ability that gives [[Absorption]] on kill, similar to the set bonus of Golem Armor.
- Golem Armor has far lower [[Effective Health]] than many other early-game armor sets available, such as [[Glacite Armor]], making it not recommended for new players.

# Golem Sword

[Item Properties]

Reforgeable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOLEM_SWORD
Tooltip: Defense: +25; Damage: +80; Strength: +125; Ability: Iron Punch RIGHT CLICK Punch the ground, damaging enemies in a hexagon around you for 250 base Magic Damage.; Mana Cost: 70 Cooldown: 3s; This item can be reforged!; RARE SWORD
The Golem Sword is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingot VIII (8)]].
The ability causes iron blocks to fall from the sky dealing AOE damage to all enemies around you.
It is an early game Sword that does similar [[Damage]] compared to an [[Aspect of the End]]. It also gives the player [[Defense]] when held, a unique stat that only this sword, the [[Earthen Blade]], the [[Wither Cloak Sword]], the [[Necromancer Sword]], and the [[Astraea]] have.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot VIII (8) ([[Iron Ingot]])|1x [[Stick]] 2x [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×2 + [[Stick]] → Golem Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
Golem Sword [[Defense]]: +25 [[Damage]]: +80 [[Strength]]: +125
[Effects]
[Ability: Iron Punch RIGHT CLICK; Punch the ground, damaging enemies in a hexagon around you for; 250 base Magic Damage (Damage Calculation).; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 70; Cooldown: 3s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.1]
## Trivia
- This is a sword that is directly related to another Hypixel minigame, [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]]. The ability of the Golem class in mega walls is the same as the item ability of the Golem Sword in SkyBlock.
- This is the one of the only swords that provides [[Defense]] with the others being [[Necromancer Sword]], [[Earthen Blade]] and [[Astraea]] with the exception of using the [[Pigman Sword|Pigman Sword's]] ability.
- This sword is generally considered useless as there are multiple swords that deal more damage at a lower cost.
- This used to be the only sword in the game that gave [[Defense]] before the dungeons update.

## Obtaining
### Crafting

## Usage
Great Carrot Candy can be consumed onto a [[Pets|Pet]] to instantly grant it 200,000 [[Pets|XP]]. Each pet can only use up to 10x [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] of any level in their lifetime.

Griffin Feather is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item found by digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Spade (disambiguation)|Spade]] during [[Diana|Diana's]] [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Obtaining
1 Griffin Feather can be obtained by digging up a [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] with a [[Spade (disambiguation)|Spade]] while [[Diana|Diana's]] Mythological Ritual perk is active:
|Spade|Chance per Treasure|Chance per Burrow No Reforge|Chance per Burrow Erudite ([[Reforging]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ancestral Spade]]|66.67% (1 in 1.5 treasures)|12.50% (1 in 8 burrows)|13.89% (1 in 7.2 burrows)|
|[[Archaic Spade]]|63.34% (1 in 1.58 treasures)|13.20% (1 in 7.58 burrows)|14.34% (1 in 6.97 burrows)|
|[[Deific Spade]]|62.71% (1 in 1.59 treasures)|13.72% (1 in 7.29 burrows)|14.11% (1 in 7.09 burrows)|

## Trivia
- Griffin Feathers cannot be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]]. If one is already recombobulated, it will be forcibly reverted to its base rarity.
- Digging up a Griffin Feather used to give the player +2.4 [[Farming|Farming XP]]. This is because the regular [[Feather]] falls under the [[Farming]] category.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Griffin Feather.|

# Grizzly Salmon
Aliases: Grizzly Bait
Categories: Rare items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Wolf Slayer, Mob Drops
[Grizzly Salmon]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GRIZZLY_BAIT
Tooltip: RARE
Grizzly Salmon is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to make the [[Hunter Ring]]. It is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from Tier IV [[Sven Packmaster|Sven Packmasters]].
## Obtaining
Grizzly Salmon has a 0.054% (7 in 13,000) drop rate from the [[Sven Packmaster]] Tier IV. It requires [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VII (7)]] to drop.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hunter Ring]]|Wolf Slayer VIII (8) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|[[Grizzly Salmon]] 1x Grizzly Salmon 1x [[Hunter Talisman]] 224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] x32 ×7 + Grizzly Salmon + [[Hunter Talisman]] → [[Hunter Ring]]|
## Trivia
- This item's name was bugged once and was incorrectly named "Rename Me".
- If this item is cooked in a furnace, it will become a cooked salmon, and if fed to the [[Siamese Lynxes]], it will disappear.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Grizzly Bait.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Renamed Grizzly Bait to Grizzly Salmon.|

It is a part of the secret [[Monster Hunter Armor|Monster Hunter]]/[[Monster Raider Armor|Raider]] sets.

# Hamster Wheel

Item ID: HAMSTER_WHEEL
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 50% for 24; hours!; RARE
The Hamster Wheel is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minion Fuel]] that is an [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from the [[Sven Packmaster]] at Tier II or higher. It boosts the speed of a [[Minions|Minion]] by 50% for 1 day.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
The Hamster Wheel drops from the [[Sven Packmaster]] at Tier II or higher.
- Tier II: 16.67% chance to obtain 1 Hamster Wheel
- Tier III: 16.59% chance to obtain 2-4 Hamster Wheels
- Tier IV: 16.49% chance to obtain 4-5 Hamster Wheels
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
The Hamster Wheel boosts the speed of a [[Minions|Minion]] by 50% for 1 day. Hamster Wheel can be placed in all [[Minions]].

The Haste Ring is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that gives permanent [[Haste Potion|Haste II]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Cobblestone VII (7) ([[Cobblestone]])|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x16 ×8 → Haste Ring|
## Usage
Accessory:
Haste Ring [[Mining Speed]]: +10
[Effects]
[Gives permanent Haste II (Haste Potion).]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Haste Artifact]]|Cobblestone IX (9) ([[Cobblestone]])|[[Haste Ring]] 1x Haste Ring 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x32 ×8 + Haste Ring → [[Haste Artifact]]|
## Trivia
- This ring is one of the couple rings that do not have a talisman version preceding it, however directly skipping to the ring tier. Other rings with the same property are the [[Devour Ring]], the [[Shady Ring]], the [[Bait Ring]], the [[Emerald Ring]], and the [[Catacombs Expert Ring]].
- Since the Dwarven Mines update, each player is given permanent Mining Fatigue on public islands, but instead of the Haste being overridden, the player is given 100 [[Mining Speed]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

|Aug 20, 2024|Lowered collection requirement from Cobblestone IX (9) ([[Cobblestone]]) to Cobblestone VII (7) ([[Cobblestone]]).|
|Aug 20, 2024|Haste Ring now grants permanent Haste II ([[Haste Potion]]), rather than Haste I ([[Haste Potion]]) for 15 seconds after breaking a block.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Haste Ring.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Haste Ring can now be upgraded to the [[Haste Artifact]].|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Lowered collection requirement from Cobblestone IX (9) ([[Cobblestone]]) to Cobblestone VII (7) ([[Cobblestone]]).|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Haste Ring now grants permanent Haste II ([[Haste Potion]]), rather than Haste I ([[Haste Potion]]) for 15 seconds after breaking a block.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Reduced craft cost from 256 (4 stacks) [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] to 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Cobblestone]].|

# Healing Tissue
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mob Drops
[Healing Tissue]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Shop]
Sell: 5,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HEALING_TISSUE
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; RARE DUNGEON ITEM
The Healing Tissue is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] (5) and above. It is used to craft the [[Florid Zombie Sword]], the upgraded version of the [[Ornate Zombie Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
[[The Watcher]], while in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] and higher floors, gives the Undeads that it spawns special properties. If the Undeads' property is Healthy, then they have a 5% chance to drop Healing Tissues.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Florid Zombie Sword]]|None|1x [[Ornate Zombie Sword]] [[Healing Tissue]] 24x Healing Tissue|Recipe: Healing Tissue x12 ×2 + [[Ornate Zombie Sword]] → [[Florid Zombie Sword]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Healing Tissue as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Added Healing Tissue.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Healing Tissue to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Healing Tissue as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

Hemoglass is a [[Rarity|RARE]] crafting material. The recipe is unlocked at [[Hemovibe|Hemovibe III (3)]], once the player has collected 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) [[Hemovibe]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Hemovibe III (3) ([[Hemovibe]])|160x [[Hemovibe]]|Recipe: [[Hemovibe]] x32 ×7 → Hemoglass|
## Usage
A Hemoglass can be consumed via right-clicking with the item in the player's hand to grant 0.5 [[Hearts]] per second for 7 seconds.
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], a Hemoglass can also be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the Hemoglass can be used similarly to when in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].

Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hewver Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Critter Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:

As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Hivethief Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Hivethief]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hivethief Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cubic Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cubic Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] on Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vampire Slayer I (1) ([[Vampire Slayer]])|16x [[Coven Seal]] 100x [[Frozen Water]]|Recipe: [[Coven Seal]] x4 ×4 + [[Frozen Water]] x20 ×5 → Holy Ice|
## Usage
Item:
Holy Ice
[Effects]
[Ability: Splash Yo Face RIGHT CLICK; For the next 1.5s, reduce Hearts damage taken by 80%.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 20; Cooldown: 4s]
## Trivia
- Holy Ice is primarily used to protect against attacks from the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] slayer boss.
  - In vampire folklore Holy Water was believed to ward off or act as a weapon against mythical evil creatures, such as vampires.
- Holy Ice is the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] equivalent of Holy Water.
  - Holy Water is water that has been sanctified by a priest for the purpose of baptism, for the blessing of persons, and as a means of repelling evil.
    - The ability name Splash Yo Face is related to the use of Holy Water in Baptisms, which are performed by sprinkling or pouring water on the head.

# Hot Chocolate Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients
[Hot Chocolate Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: Chocolate Shop ([[Chocolate Factory]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOT_CHOCOLATE_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain +15 Pet Luck and +0.05x Chocolate per second.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); ❣ Requires Chocolate Factory VI.; RARE
A Hot Chocolate Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
Up to 4 Hot Chocolate Mixin can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] for 1,500,000,000 [[Chocolate]] each upon reaching Chocolate Factory VI.
## Usage
### Brewing
A Hot Chocolate Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +15 [[Pet Luck]] and [[Chocolate|+0.05x Chocolate]] per second. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Hot Chocolate Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## History

|Nov 13, 2024|Added Hot Chocolate Mixin.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 19, 2024|0.20.8|Added Hot Chocolate Mixin.|

[Item Metadata]

Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants a +20% chance to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures while fishing in a Hotspot.; RARE BAIT
Hotspot Bait is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Bait]] that boosts the player's chance to fish up [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] Sea Creatures.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] 1x [[Scorched Crab Stick]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] + [[Scorched Crab Stick]] → Hotspot Bait x32|
## Usage
### Fishing
Hotspot Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Hotspot Bait will be consumed to grant a +20% chance to catch [[Sea Creatures|Fishing Hotspot Sea Creatures]]. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
## Trivia
- The raw materials of the Hotspot Bait recipe sell for a total of 27,400 [[Coins]] to merchant NPCs, while the [[Hotspot Bait]] 32 Hotspot Baits sell for 160,000 [[Coins]].

# Hotspot Tonic Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Confirmations needed, Brewing ingredients, Mixins
[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 64x [[Half-Eaten Mushroom]] 8x [[Ink Splat]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOTSPOT_TONIC_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Grants +10% chance to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures while fishing in a Hotspot.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); RARE
A Hotspot Tonic Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[Ink Splat]] 64x [[Half-Eaten Mushroom]]|Recipe: [[Half-Eaten Mushroom]] x16 ×4 + [[Ink Splat]] x8 → Hotspot Tonic Mixin|
## Usage
### Brewing
A Hotspot Tonic Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player a +10% chance to catch [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] [[Sea Creatures]] while fishing in a [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Hotspot Tonic Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Bugs
- The Hotspot Tonic Mixin does not apply in the [[Torrhus Canyon]]'s [[Hotspot Haven]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Added Hotspot Tonic Mixin.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|May 26, 2026|0.25|Added Hotspot Tonic Mixin.|

The Hummingbird Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Chop]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Tree Gifts
A Hummingbird Shard has can be rarely obtained from [[Tree Gifts]].
|Tree Type|Chance|
|-|-|
|Fig Tree ([[Fig Log]])|0.15% (1 in 666 gifts)|
|Mangrove Tree ([[Mangrove Log]])|0.25% (1 in 400 gifts)|
|Helix Tree ([[Helix Log]])|0.3% (1 in 333 gifts)|
### Fusion
A Hummingbird Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hummingbird Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Chop]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Chop ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +10%–100% chance to Chop an extra log, on top of the ones you swept.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Hummingbird Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Hummingbird Shard has the following description:
- Its wings beat so quickly that they leave streaks of color in the air, like brushstrokes on the wind.
## Trivia
- [[Attributes|Chop]] is the shortest [[Attributes|Attribute]] name alongside [[Attributes|Veil]] ( [[Ghost Shard]]) and [[Attributes|Pity]] ( [[Daemon Shard]]).
- The extra log chopped does not allow the player to bypass the cap of 35 swept logs on the [[Hub Island]] and [[The Park]].
  - It does bypass the cap of 20 swept logs on [[Galatea]].

# Hunting Toolkit
Categories: Rare items, Items, Pages with UIs
[Hunting Toolkit]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HUNTING_TOOLKIT
Tooltip: Consumable; Store all of your Hunting Tools in one convenient place and swap between them with ease.; Right-click to consume!; RARE
A Hunting Toolkit is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can hold [[Hunting]] [[Tools]].
## Obtaining
A Hunting Toolkit can be purchased for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] from [[David Hunterborough]].
## Usage
A Hunting Toolkit can be consumed once to permanently unlock the Hunting Toolkit menu and gain [[SkyBlock Levels|2 SkyBlock XP]].
The Hunting Toolkit menu can be opened in the player's [[Storage]] or with the /huntingtoolkit command.
It can store one of each [[Hunting]] [[Tools|Tool]] and 5 [[Huntraps]].
|Type|Items|
|-|-|
|[[Huntaxes]]|[[Worn Huntaxe - Genesis]] [[Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus]] [[Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus]] [[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]] [[Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum]]|
|[[Black Holes]]|[[Small Pocket Black Hole]] [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]] [[Large Pocket Black Hole]]|
|[[Lassos]]|[[Abysmal Lasso]] [[Vinerip Lasso]] [[Entangler Lasso]] [[Everstretch Lasso]]|
|[[Fishing Nets]]|[[Basic Fishing Net]] [[Medium Fishing Net]] [[Turbo Fishing Net]] [[Gigantic Fishing Net]]|
|[[Huntraps]]|[[Small Huntrap]] [[Medium Huntrap]] [[Large Huntrap]] [[Greater Huntrap]] [[Astral Huntrap]]|
## Tips
- If used often, [[Fishing Nets]] and [[Huntraps]] may want to be stored somewhere else than a Hunting Toolkit.
  - When [[Fishing Nets]] are used in the water, it is possible to accidentaly shift and right-click to open the Hunting Toolkit.
  - Having a [[Huntraps|Huntrap]] placed in the Hunting Toolkit places it back inside when collecting, resulting in the player having to open the menu to place them back down to collect more [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]].
## Trivia
- Trying to modify a Hunting Tool through [[The Hex]] while it is still in the Hunting Toolkit displays the following message:
The Hex is displeased!
You have to take this item out of the
Hunting Toolkit to modify it!

|Apr 21, 2026|Removed Hunting Toolkit from [[Museum]].|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Hunting Toolkit as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

The Inferno Fuel Block is a [[Rarity|RARE]] crafting item that is only used to craft [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
An Inferno Fuel Block can be purchased from the [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]] for 75 [[Bits]] or 65 [[Bits]] if the player has [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL IX (9)]].
It can also be purchased for 56.25 [[Bits]] each when getting 64 for 3,600 [[Bits]], or 48.75 [[Bits]] each when getting 64 for 3,120 [[Bits]] with [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL IX (9)]].

# Inferno Koi Shard
Aliases: Inferno Koi
Categories: Rare items, Shards
[Inferno Koi Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Shard ID: R59
Families: Treasure Fish ([[Shard Families]])
Skill: Global
Category: Water
[Attribute]
Name: Crimson Serendipity ([[Attributes]])
Effect: Increase the odds of finding a [[Vanquisher]] by +2%–20%.
Requirement: Hunting X (10) ([[Hunting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: INFERNO_KOI
Tooltip: Crimson Serendipity I Increase the odds of finding a Vanquisher by +2%.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R59)
The Inferno Koi Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Crimson Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
An Inferno Koi Shard can be obtained from fishing in the [[Crimson Isle]] with [[Wooden Bait]]. There is a 100% (1 in 1 rolls) chance for [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] drops to be replaced with one.
### Huntraps
An Inferno Koi Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Crimson Isle]] (Under lava).
### Fusion
An Inferno Koi Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Inferno Koi Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Crimson Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Crimson Serendipity ([[Attributes]]); Increase the odds of finding a [[Vanquisher]] by +2%–20%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Inferno Koi Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Inferno Koi Shard has the following description:
- These koi are often seen basking near lava flows, absorbing the heat into their vibrant scales.

# InfiniPot™
Aliases: Infinipot, Infinipot™
Categories: Rare items, Accessories, Catacombs, Dungeons, Tools, Other Tools, Aura's Mayorship, Dungeon Items
[InfiniPot™]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Malik]]
Buy: 5,000 (78 stacks plus 8) [[Wither Essence]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INFINIPOT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Upon starting a floor in The Catacombs, automatically consumes the selected Dungeon Potion from your inventory or Potion Bag.; If you do not have the selected potion in your inventory or Potion Bag, it will be purchased and consumed automatically using Coins from your purse or bank.; Selected Potion: Dungeon I Potion Left-click to select a potion!; Enabled: On Right-click to toggle!; “Alixer’s latest and greatest creation!”; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE DUNGEON ACCESSORY
The InfiniPot™ is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It consumes [[Dungeon Potion|Dungeon Potions]] in the player's inventory or [[Potion Bag]], and consumes [[Coins]] if the player do not have any selected [[Dungeon Potion]].
## Obtaining
The InfiniPot™ can be obtained from the [[Essence Shops|Wither Essence Shop]] from [[Malik]] for 5,000 [[Wither Essence]].
## Usage
Accessory:
InfiniPot™
[Effects]
[Upon starting a floor in The Catacombs, automatically consumes the selected Dungeon Potion from your inventory.; If you do not have the selected potion in your inventory, it will be purchased and consumed automatically using Coins from your purse or bank.]
The InfiniPot™ automatically consumes the selected tier of [[Dungeon Potion]] in the player's inventory upon starting a [[Catacombs]] run. If the player does not have the selected potion in the inventory, they will automatically gain the effect and be deducted the equivalent amount of [[Coins]] instead. This effect can be toggled ON or OFF, both in the inventory or in the [[Accessory Bag]].
## Game Messages

|-|-|

|Entering The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]) with an active Infinipot ([[InfiniPot™]]) and the selected potion in the inventory or [[Potion Bag]]|Your InfiniPot™ consumed a Dungeon I Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ consumed a Dungeon II Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ consumed a Dungeon III Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ consumed a Dungeon IV Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ consumed a Dungeon V Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ consumed a Dungeon VI Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ consumed a Dungeon VII Potion!|

|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ purchased and consumed a Dungeon II Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ purchased and consumed a Dungeon III Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ purchased and consumed a Dungeon IV Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ purchased and consumed a Dungeon V Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ purchased and consumed a Dungeon VI Potion!|
|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ purchased and consumed a Dungeon VII Potion!|

|(same as above)|Your InfiniPot™ tried to purchase a Dungeon VII Potion but failed due to insufficient balance!|
## Trivia
- The InfiniPot™ was designed by 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] rembutquaglet for the [[Minister Election]].
- The mentioned [[Alixer]], an NPC that grants potion buffs to [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] players, is mentioned in the item's description.
- The [[Coins]] paid for the potions can be reduced by a [[Shady Ring]], a [[Crooked Artifact]], or a [[Seal of the Family]].

## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Invisibug Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] an [[Invisibug]].
### Floor Drops
An Invisibug Shard can be obtained from [[Floor Drops]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It has a 0.94% (1 in 106 rolls) chance to drop from one.
### Huntraps
An Invisibug Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Moonglade Marsh]] (On Land).
### Fusion
An Invisibug Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

# Island NPC
Aliases: Island NPCs
Categories: Rare items, Items, Pages with UIs, Social, Non-player characters, Decoration
[Island NPC]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Social V (5) ([[Social]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Amelia]]
Buy: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ISLAND_NPC
Tooltip: Spawn island NPCs for your private world! Right-Click one to edit its settings and add dialogue.; ❣ Requires Social Skill 5.; RARE
The Island NPC is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be placed on a player's [[Private Island]], and are entirely decorative. When guests visit, they can interact with the NPCs, which speaks their dialogue into their guest's chat.
## Obtaining
Island NPC can be purchased from [[Amelia]] for 50,000 [[Coins]], once players are [[Social|Social V (5)]].
## Usage
When placed on a player's [[Private Island]], the owner can right-click to open the GUI. From there, several options can be set.
### Select NPC Type
This option allows for selection between available Minecraft mobs, and the unique "Player" type. Players can pick between the following NPC types:
Show/Hide NPC List
|NPC|Image|
|-|-|
|Player|Varies|
|Villager||
|Cow||
|Pig||
|Sheep||
|Snowman||
|Iron Golem||
|Witch||
|Blaze||
|Cave Spider||
|Creeper||
|Guardian||
|Enderman||
|Zombie||
|Zombie Pigman||
|Magma Cube||
|Skeleton||
|Slime||
#### Unique NPC Options
If island owners select either the "Player", "Villager" or "Pig" mob type, an additional option appears underneath "Select NPC Name".
For "Player", this option allows island owners to pick from one of 51 unique skins created by the Hypixel server. Players cannot select their own custom skins for this purpose. Below is a list of all of the skin's names.
- Adventurer
- Artist
- Baker
- Beekeeper
- Bouncer
- Butcher
- Casual Guy
- Gamer
- Ghost Tee
- Golfer
- Delivery Boy
- Red Hoodie
- Steve
- Summer Boy
- Treasure Steve
- Yellow Hoodie
- Alex
- Beach Party Girl
- Blue Bird
- Candy Corn
- Casual Girl
- Emo Girl
- Flower Girl
- Red Girl
- Lemon Girl
- Lumberjack Girl
- Lunar
- Oceana
- Purple Bunny
- Pink Girl
- Red Heart
- Roses
- Summer Girl
- Trendy Girl
- Unicorn Girl
- Yellow Top
- Drowned
- Husk
- Iron Golem
- Pillager
- Wandering Trader
- Cosmos
- Dark Knight
- Dark Space Trooper
- Fancy Jerry
- Gray Knight
- Hercules
- Heroic Jerry
- Phoenix
- Shaman
- Solar
For "Villager", this option allows island owners to pick from the five villager professions, changing the color of the Villager's clothes.
For "Pig", this option allows island owners to toggle saddle on the pig's back.
Additional skins can be achieved by using [[Ditto Skin]] to capture a player's skin.
### Select NPC Name
This option allows for renaming of the NPC. The name must be short enough to fit onto a Minecraft Sign - a maximum of 15 characters. This is always 15 characters regardless of Minecraft version, despite Minecraft now allowing signs of indefinite length.
### Edit Dialogue
This option allows the owner to add, remove, and change the dialogue shown to island guests. Once players initialize Dialogue Mode, they are able to use Minecraft's chat function to change the text available. Island owners can see how their own dialogue will be displayed by punching/left-clicking the NPC in the gameworld.
The "/dialogue" command has 4 uses:
- "/dialogue list" returns a list of all dialogue that NPC currently has. This is also visible on the tooltip of the "Edit Dialogue" option in the Island NPC menu.
- "/dialogue add <text>" allows island owners to input the next line of text. This will simply add the text in the command into the next line of dialogue.
- "/dialogue set <line> <text>" allows island owners to change an existing line of text. The "<line>" parameter requires a number input to identify which line of text the island owner wishes to change.
- "/dialogue clear" clears all existing dialogue. This can also be done by removing and replacing the NPC.
Players can leave Dialogue Mode by right-clicking on the same Island NPC they were just editing.
The maximum allowed lines of dialogue is 100.
#### Color Modifiers
The possible colors available to Island NPCs' Dialogue.
Using the '&' symbol on an NPC's dialogue, followed by a number between '0' and '9', or a letter between 'a' and 'f' will change the color of the text immediately after the identifier. This also works for NPC names.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|On Interaction; Dialogue Unset|[NPC] Untitled NPC: yo wassup bruh|
|On Interaction; Dialogue Unset|[NPC] Untitled NPC: bloop|
|On Interaction; Dialogue Unset|[NPC] Untitled NPC: Hi there, welcome to my island!|
|On Interaction; Dialogue Unset|[NPC] Untitled NPC: I am a magical NPC. Thank you for clicking!|
## Trivia
- "Player", "Villager" and "Pig" are the only NPC types that has more options available. While many other mobs have variants in the game (like child mobs, or slime sizes)
  - Only Villager professions and player skins are selectable and pig saddle is toggleable.
- Island NPCs can be used to tell the story of a players' island without the need for ugly signs! They can also be used for announcements, as it's common for popular islands to place an NPC near spawn with quick information about where you are and why that island exists.
- There is no curse filter on Island NPCs.
- The limit to the amount of Island NPCs placeable on a single island is 21. Attempting to place an NPC when this limit has already been reached will result in the spawn egg being consumed, but no NPC will appear. This means that the spawn egg will be permanently deleted.
- Players can be kicked for spam if they use the "/dialogue" command many times in rapid succession. This will evacuate them out of SkyBlock and into a Hypixel lobby.

Certain materials attract certain types of  Pests.; Selected Nozzle: Juicy Selected Material: Compost Attracts: Earthworm, Mosquito; Ability: Spray RIGHT CLICK Sprays your selected material onto the Garden plot you are on. Lasts for 45m.; Juicy Nozzle Bonus:; ➜ +50 Bonus Pest Chance while in a sprayed plot.; ➜  Pests are 2x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.; Cooldown: 3s; ❣ Requires Garden Level 5.; RARE
The Juicy Sprayonator is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
The Juicy Sprayonator can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 1 [[Sprayonator]], 1 [[Juicy Nozzle]] and 1,000 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Sprayonator:
Juicy Sprayonator
[Effects]
[Ability: Configure LEFT CLICK; Select a nozzle and material for spraying. Certain materials attract certain types of Pest (Pest (Mob Type)). Ability: Spray RIGHT CLICK; Sprays your selected material onto the Garden plot you are on. Lasts for 45m.; ➜ +50 Bonus Pest Chance while in a sprayed plot.; ➜ Pest (Pest (Mob Type)) are 2x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.; Cooldown: 3s]
The Juicy Sprayonator can be used to spray a [[The Garden|Garden]] plot for 45 minutes using [[Compost]], [[Plant Matter]], [[Dung]], [[Honey Jar]], [[Tasty Cheese]], or [[Jelly]].
### Attracting Pests
The Juicy Sprayonator can be used with the appropriate materials to attract certain [[Pests]] to plots. When a spray is active, [[Pests]] are guaranteed to spawn in the plot the player is farming in, or one of the four directly adjacent plots.
The materials below can be used to attract specific pests:
|Material|Attracted Pests|
|-|-|
|[[Compost]]|[[Earthworm]], [[Mosquito]]|
|[[Plant Matter]]|[[Locust]], [[Slug]]|
|[[Dung]]|[[Beetle]], [[Fly]]|
|[[Honey Jar]]|[[Moth]], [[Cricket]]|
|[[Tasty Cheese]]|[[Rat]], [[Mite]]|
|[[Jelly]]|[[Praying Mantis]], [[Dragonfly]], [[Firefly]]|
The weights of the targeted [[Pests]] are increased by +1,000, on top of the base 100 weight.
### Nozzles
Additional effects are applied in sprayed plots depending on the selected nozzle:
- Basic Nozzle: Consumes 1 of the material. Grants +25 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 1.5x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
- [[Juicy Nozzle]]: Consumes 3 of the material. Grants +50 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 2x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
## History

The Jump Elixir is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
The Jump Elixir can be purchased from [[Shifted]] for 5 [[Shy Crux|Shy Cruxes]] and 5,000 [[Motes]].
## Usage
The player can consume a Jump Elixir to grant [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost I]] for 0:20, and is the only way to gain the effect in the [[Rift Dimension]] since [[Potions]] cannot be transferred.
## Notes
- The effect doesn't show up in the Effects menu.

# Kelvin Inverter
Categories: Rare items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Blaze Slayer, Mob Drops
[Kelvin Inverter]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]])
Source: [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier II+
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KELVIN_INVERTER
Tooltip: Drops rarely from the Inferno Demonlord.; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Kelvin Inverter is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. It is obtained as a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier II+ with at least [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer III (3)]].
## Obtaining
The Kelvin Inverter can be dropped from Tier II and higher Inferno Demonlords, if the player has [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL III (3)]] or higher.
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 2.82%
- Tier III: 2.70%
- Tier IV: 2.54%
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Kelvin Inverter]] 1x Kelvin Inverter 1x [[Inferno Vertex]] 3x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 8x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x2 ×4 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] ×3 + [[Inferno Vertex]] + Kelvin Inverter → [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|Blaze Slayer V (5) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Kelvin Inverter]] 6x Kelvin Inverter 8x [[Inferno Vertex]] 12x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 48x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Inferno Vertex]] x2 ×4 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x6 ×2 + [[Molten Powder]] x24 ×2 + Kelvin Inverter x6 → [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|
|[[Entropy Suppressor]]|Chili Pepper IV (4) ([[Chili Pepper]])|[[Kelvin Inverter]] 4x Kelvin Inverter 16x [[Heavy Gabagool]] 32x [[Chili Pepper]]|Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] x8 ×4 + [[Heavy Gabagool]] x4 ×4 + Kelvin Inverter x4 → [[Entropy Suppressor]]|
## Trivia
The Kelvin Inverter references the the base unit for temperature in the International System of Units, the Kelvin, because of the fact that it is used in the recipe for the [[Entropy Suppressor]], where Entropy means the quantity representing the unavailability of a system's thermal energy for conversion into mechanical work, and Suppressor means something to prevent something from happening (whereas here preventing the unavailability of a system's thermal energy).
## History

|Mar 21, 2022|Added Kelvin Inverter.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Kelvin Inverter as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Kelvin Inverter.|
|Aug 5, 2022|Now sells to NPCs for 100,000 [[Coins]].|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Kelvin Inverter to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Kelvin Inverter as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

The Key to Infested House Soul is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item found in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
## Obtaining
A Key to Infested House Soul can be purchased from [[Kat (Rift)|Neuroscientist Kat]] for 500 [[Motes]] after having deposited 40 Silverfish in the [[Kat (Rift)|Vermin Bin]] with a [[Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner]].
## Usage
Once the player has obtained a Key to Infested House Soul, they may claim the [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] located in the [[Infested House]] at [[Coordinates|-34, 71, -88]].

# Kuudra Tentacle
Aliases: Kuudra Tentacle (item), Kuudra Tentacles, Kuudra's Tentacle, Kuudra's Tentacles
Categories: Rare items, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Kuudra Tentacle]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_TENTACLE
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
A Kuudra Tentacle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item in paid [[Kuudra]] chests.
## Obtaining
### Kuudra
A Kuudra Tentacle can appear in the secondary loot pool with 16 weight in the Paid Chest of any tier of [[Kuudra]], making the drop chance approximately as follows:
|Tier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier 1 (Basic)|17.50% (1 in 5.72 chests)|
|Tier 2 (Hot)|16.04% (1 in 6.23 chests)|
|Tier 3 (Burning)|11.13% (1 in 8.99 chests)|
|Tier 4 (Fiery)|9.22% (1 in 10.85 chests)|
|Tier 5 (Infernal)|8.61% (1 in 11.61 chests)|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Kuudra's Kidney]]|None|[[Kuudra Tentacle]] 9x Kuudra Tentacle|Recipe: Kuudra Tentacle ×9 → [[Kuudra's Kidney]]|
|[[Kuudra's Lung]]|None|1x [[Kuudra's Kidney]] [[Kuudra Tentacle]] 16x Kuudra Tentacle|Recipe: Kuudra Tentacle x2 ×8 + [[Kuudra's Kidney]] → [[Kuudra's Lung]]|
|[[Kuudra's Heart]]|None|1x [[Kuudra's Lung]] [[Kuudra Tentacle]] 32x Kuudra Tentacle|Recipe: Kuudra Tentacle x4 ×8 + [[Kuudra's Lung]] → [[Kuudra's Heart]]|
## Notes
- A total of [[Kuudra Tentacle]] 57 Kuudra Tentacles are needed for Kuudra's Heart.
## Trivia
- The Kuudra Tentacle was created by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Future77 after becoming a [[Minister Election|Minister]] for [[Candidate Aura|Aura]].

## Obtaining
### Fusion

- Second: [[Invisibug Shard]]

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Ladybug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Pretty Clothes]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Pretty Clothes ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% more [[Copper]] from Garden Visitors ([[The Garden]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Next →: [[Greater Huntrap]]
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 164x (2 stacks plus 36 (164 in total)) [[Tender Wood]] 52x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]
Raw Materials to upgrade: 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Tender Wood]] 32x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RETIA_ROBUSTA
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Retentio RIGHT CLICK Traps can be placed near the spawn location of a Shard creature, with time it will eventually catch it.; Ability: Robusta Reduces the time to capture by 20%.; In robore, fiducia.; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE TRAP
The Large Huntrap is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Huntraps|Huntrap]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Tender Wood|Tender Wood V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Tender Wood V (5) ([[Tender Wood]])|1x [[Medium Huntrap]] 32x [[Enchanted Fig Log]] 128x [[Tender Wood]]|Recipe: [[Tender Wood]] x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x8 ×4 + [[Medium Huntrap]] → Large Huntrap|
## Usage
A Large Huntrap can be placed near the spawn location of any creature that can drop a [[Attributes|Shard]] to collect one. The time to catch a shard is random and based on the rarity of the shard caught (see [[Huntraps|Huntraps#Time]]).
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Greater Huntrap]]|Tender Wood VII (7) ([[Tender Wood]])|[[Large Huntrap]] 1x Large Huntrap 64x [[Enchanted Fig Log]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Tender Wood]]|Recipe: [[Tender Wood]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x16 ×4 + Large Huntrap → [[Greater Huntrap]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntrap's description, "In robore, fiducia." is Latin, and means "In strength, confidence."

The Lava Sea Creature Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor]] set composed of the [[Taurus Helmet]], the [[Flaming Chestplate]], the [[Moogma Leggings]], and the [[Slug Boots]]. There is no Full Set Bonus to this set. Armor pieces in this set have different [[Fishing]] level requirements.
## Obtaining
Every piece of the set has a 2% (1 in 50) to be obtained from its associated [[Crimson Isle]] Sea Creature.
- [[Taurus Helmet]] can be dropped from the [[Taurus]].
- [[Flaming Chestplate]] can be dropped from the [[Lava Flame]].
- [[Moogma Leggings]] can be dropped from the [[Moogma]].
- [[Slug Boots]] can be dropped from the [[Magma Slug]].

|[[Moogma Leggings]]; [[Health]]: +200 [[Defense]]: +175 [[Crit Damage]]: +15 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +3 Damage taken from [[Magmatic]] mobs is reduced by 5% and damage dealt is increased by 1.1x.|[[Slug Boots]]; [[Health]]: +150 [[Defense]]: +125 [[Strength]]: +20 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +3 Ability: Bouncy SNEAK; When falling from a great height, you'll bounce straight up!|
[Total]
[Lava Sea Creature Armor Health: +750 Defense: +650 Strength: +45 Crit Damage: +35 Sea Creature Chance: +12]
### Brewing
Recipe: [[Decoy]]/[[Inflatable Jerry]]/[[Spirit Leap]]/[[Trap]] → [[Secret I Potion]], [[Secret I Potion]], [[Secret I Potion]]
Lava Sea Creature Armor can be worn to brew a [[Secret I Potion|Secret I]] [[Potions|Potion]].
## Trivia
- Lava Sea Creature Armor grants the third highest amount of base [[Sea Creature Chance]] in the game at 3% per piece and 12% total, only rivalled by [[Thunder Armor]] and [[Magma Lord Armor]].

# Livid Fragment
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Items
[Livid Fragment]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dungeon Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LIVID_FRAGMENT
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; RARE DUNGEON ITEM
Livid Fragment is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] item. It can be used to upgrade certain [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] items.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Livid Fragment have a 50% chance to drop from a [[Livid]] summoned by [[The Watcher]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]]. This is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv275-545]|[[Livid (Watcher)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
## Usage
### Crafting
Livid Fragment can be used to upgrade various items to grant them extra stats.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Shadow Assassin Helmet ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]])|None|1x Shadow Assassin Helmet ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) [[Livid Fragment]] 8x Livid Fragment|Recipe: Livid Fragment ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]])|None|1x Shadow Assassin Chestplate ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) [[Livid Fragment]] 8x Livid Fragment|Recipe: Livid Fragment ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|Shadow Assassin Leggings ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]])|None|1x Shadow Assassin Leggings ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) [[Livid Fragment]] 8x Livid Fragment|Recipe: Livid Fragment ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|Shadow Assassin Boots ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]])|None|1x Shadow Assassin Boots ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) [[Livid Fragment]] 8x Livid Fragment|Recipe: Livid Fragment ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] → [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|[[Last Breath]]|None|1x [[Last Breath]] [[Livid Fragment]] 8x Livid Fragment|Recipe: Livid Fragment ×8 + [[Last Breath]] → [[Last Breath]]|
|[[Shadow Fury]]|None|1x [[Shadow Fury]] [[Livid Fragment]] 8x Livid Fragment|Recipe: Livid Fragment ×8 + [[Shadow Fury]] → [[Shadow Fury]]|
|[[Shadow Assassin Cloak]]|None|1x [[Shadow Assassin Cloak]] [[Livid Fragment]] 8x Livid Fragment|Recipe: Livid Fragment ×8 + [[Shadow Assassin Cloak]] → [[Shadow Assassin Cloak]]|
## Trivia
- It takes a total of 56 Livid Fragments to upgrade every item.
- Livid Fragments are the only fragment that can upgrade one of every combat gear ([[Bows]], [[Equipment]], [[Swords|Sword]], and [[Armor]] ).
- Surprisingly, the [[Livid Dagger]] cannot be upgraded with Livid Fragments.
## History

|Jun 4, 2024|Moved Livid Fragment to a new section in Bazaar.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 1, 2020|0.9.4|Added Livid Fragment.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Livid Fragment to the [[Bazaar]].|
