# Living Metal Heart
Categories: Rare items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Living Metal Heart Collection, Collections
[Living Metal Heart]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 250 [[Motes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: METAL_HEART
Tooltip: Collection Item; Something about DNA...; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Living Metal Heart is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] [[Collections|Collection]] Item.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|1|[[Snake-in-a-Boot]] Recipe|+4|
|II|10|[[Living Metal Anchor]] Recipe|+4|
|III|25|[[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|50|[[Bluetooth Ring]] Recipe|+4|
|V|100|[[Polarvoid Book]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Living Metal Heart can be obtained by breaking all the blocks that formed a Living Metal Snake by using a [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]], [[Anti-Sentient Pickaxe]], [[Eon Pickaxe]], or [[Chrono Pickaxe]] after having used a [[Frozen Water Pungi]] on it.
The player who mines the snake's head, as well as any players who used a [[Frozen Water Pungi]] on the snake's head, both receive a Living Metal Heart.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Leggings of the Coven]]|Vampire Slayer III (3) ([[Vampire Slayer]])|1x [[Femurgrowth Leggings]] 1x Leggings of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]]) 1x [[Bloodbadge]] [[Living Metal Heart]] 24x Living Metal Heart|Recipe: [[Femurgrowth Leggings]] + [[Bloodbadge]] + [[Armor of the Pack]] + Living Metal Heart x6 ×4 → [[Leggings of the Coven]]|
|[[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|None|1x [[Orange Chestplate]] 12x [[Bloodbadge]] 24x [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] [[Living Metal Heart]] 64x Living Metal Heart|Recipe: Living Metal Heart x32 ×2 + [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] x6 ×4 + [[Orange Chestplate]] + [[Bloodbadge]] x12 → [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|
|[[Weirder Tuba]]|Vampire Slayer IV (4) ([[Vampire Slayer]]) Wolf Slayer V (5) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Weird Tuba]] 1x [[Bloodbadge]] [[Living Metal Heart]] 16x Living Metal Heart|Recipe: Living Metal Heart x4 ×4 + [[Weird Tuba]] + [[Bloodbadge]] → [[Weirder Tuba]]|
|[[Snake-in-a-Boot]]|Living Metal Heart I (1)|1x [[Exceedingly Comfy Sneakers]] [[Living Metal Heart]] 15x Living Metal Heart|Recipe: Living Metal Heart x5 ×3 + [[Exceedingly Comfy Sneakers]] → [[Snake-in-a-Boot]]|
|[[Living Metal Anchor]]|Living Metal Heart II (2)|[[Living Metal Heart]] 9x Living Metal Heart 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Living Metal]]|Recipe: Living Metal Heart x3 ×3 + [[Living Metal]] x32 ×4 → [[Living Metal Anchor]]|
|[[Bluetooth Ring]]|Living Metal Heart IV (4)|[[Living Metal Heart]] 32x Living Metal Heart|Recipe: Living Metal Heart x4 ×8 → [[Bluetooth Ring]]|
|[[Polarvoid Book]]|Living Metal Heart V (5)|3x [[Frosty Crux]] [[Living Metal Heart]] 20x Living Metal Heart|Recipe: [[Frosty Crux]] ×3 + Living Metal Heart x20 → [[Polarvoid Book]]|
|[[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]]|Living Metal Heart III (3)|[[Living Metal Heart]] 1x Living Metal Heart 8x [[Living Metal]]|Recipe: [[Living Metal]] ×8 + Living Metal Heart → [[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]]|
## Tips
- While Living Metal Hearts are intended to be farmed together by at least two players, it is possible to do it alone. The easiest way to do this is to hold right click, calm the snake using a [[Frozen Water Pungi]], and then quickly swap to a pickaxe and mine a block, while still holding right click in order to be able to quickly swap back to the Frozen Water Pungi. There is a grace period of a few seconds before the snake starts moving again. This process can be repeated for each block until the snake's head is mined.
  - When done this way, the player will still only receive one Living Metal Heart per snake, rather than two.
  - The [[Anti-Sentient Pickaxe]] cannot be used while solo, because it takes too long to break the head of the snake.
- The best tool to use is the [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]], as it breaks the head of the snake fastest. The rest of the snake is broken at equal speeds regardless of the tool used.
## Gallery
- An example of a living metal snake

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Lizard King Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Lizard Family
- Second: [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] or higher and Water Category
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Lizard King Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Great Karma]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Great Karma ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Hunting Fortune]] towards RARE Attributes.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Magic Jellybean
Aliases: Jellybean
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Magic Jellybean]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MAGIC_JELLYBEAN
Tooltip: A strange bean that starts growing in unexpected ways, stretching higher and higher.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Magic Jellybean is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Magic Jellybean can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 5 [[Sugar Cane]] and 3 [[Duskbloom]] within 1 block of [[Sand]].
While growing, a Magic Jellybean needs water and has 120 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases [[Farming]] experience by 30% and decreases yield by -20%.
It can be harvested starting from the 12th Growth Stage. Every 12 stages after that will increase its drop multiplier by +1, up to a maximum of 10x. This can be observed on the [[Plant Diagnostics Tool]].
### Drops
When harvested, Magic Jellybean also drops 600 (9 stacks plus 24) [[Moonflower]], 600 [[Sunflower]], 1,200 (18 stacks plus 48) [[Sugar Cane]], and +4,800 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Magic Jellybean can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 160,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 80 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Magic Jellybean grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Magic Jellybeans are used to create a [[Chorus Fruit]] and [[All-in Aloe]].
## Tips
- Planting a full plot consisting of [[Magic Jellybean]] 16 Magic Jellybeans and harvesting them at the 36th Growth Stage grants enough [[Magic Jellybean]] Magic Jellybeans to comfortably complete [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestones]], obtain a [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]], as well as [[Timestalk Clone]] [[Bestiary]].
## Trivia
- Due to its height of 10 blocks, in certain locations with certain [[Greenhouse Skins]], including the basic one, Magic Jellybean can grow through the roof.
- Magic Jellybeans do not decay when fully grown or placed by the player.
- A block cannot be placed between two Magic Jellybeans if one or more Magic Jellybean is stage two or below.
## Gallery
- Magic Jellybean in the Greenhouse Magic Jellybean in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Magic Jellybean.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Magic Jellybean.|

# Mana Disintegrator
Categories: Rare items, Items, Slayer, Blaze Slayer, Mob Drops
[Mana Disintegrator]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Blaze Slayer II (2) ([[Blaze Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MANA_DISINTEGRATOR
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Applies ᛃ on wands or deployables, reducing their mana cost by 1%. Can be applied up to 10 times.; RARE
Mana Disintegrator is a [[Rarity|RARE]] upgrade item from [[Blaze Slayer]]. It reduces mana cost of deployables and wands.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Mana Disintegrator is a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drops from [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier II+ upon reaching [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer II (2)]].
## Usage
It can be used in an [[Anvil]] with any deployable or wand. This reduces their [[Mana]] cost by 1%. After combining, it is consumed.
Up to 10 Mana Disintegrators can be applied to an item, reducing its mana cost up to 10%.
In the case of [[Power Orbs]], the mana cost is reduced by 2% per level up to 20%. (This appears as a decrease from 50% to 40% of the user's maximum mana.)
The following items can be upgraded with Mana Disintegrators:
|Slot|Item|
|-|-|
|[[Radiant Power Orb]]|[[Radiant Power Orb]]|
|[[Mana Flux Power Orb]]|[[Mana Flux Power Orb]]|
|[[Overflux Power Orb]]|[[Overflux Power Orb]]|
|[[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]|[[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]|
|[[Warning Flare]]|[[Warning Flare]]|
|[[Alert Flare]]|[[Alert Flare]]|
|[[SOS Flare]]|[[SOS Flare]]|
|[[Dwarven Lantern]]|[[Dwarven Lantern]]|
|[[Mithril Lantern]]|[[Mithril Lantern]]|
|[[Titanium Lantern]]|[[Titanium Lantern]]|
|[[Glacite Lantern]]|[[Glacite Lantern]]|
|[[Will-o'-wisp]]|[[Will-o'-wisp]]|
|[[Umberella]]|[[Umberella]]|
|[[Totem of Corruption]]|[[Totem of Corruption]]|
|[[Fire Veil Wand]]|[[Fire Veil Wand]]|
|[[Staff of the Volcano]]|[[Staff of the Volcano]]|
|[[Gyrokinetic Wand]]|[[Gyrokinetic Wand]]|
## Notes
- The effect stacks multiplicatively with the [[Attributes|Breeze]] attribute, for a total reduction of 19%.
- It also stacks multiplicatively with [[Wise Dragon Armor]]'s set bonus, for a total reduction of 40%.
- While working properly on [[Totem of Corruption]], the reduced mana cost won't be shown on the tooltip, as it is with other items.
## Trivia
- Mana Disintegrators can be applied to the [[Small Pocket Black Hole|Small]] and [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]], but have no effect as they have no mana cost.
- The above also applies to the [[Hollow Wand]], which consumes Spirit ⚶ stacks instead of mana. (verify)
## History

|Sep 11, 2024|Added Mana Disintegrator to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Mar 23, 2026|Mana Disintegrator can now be sold to NPCs for 1,000 [[Coins]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

Tooltip: Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 1m buffing up to 5; players within 18 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max Vitality Cost: 50; Orb Buff: Mana Flux • Heal yourself for 40❤ per second.; • Heal others for 20❤ per second.; • Gain +50 Health Regen.; • Grants +50% base mana regen.; • Grants +10 Strength.; Only one deployable buff applies.; ☠ Requires Wolf Slayer 6.; RARE DEPLOYABLE
The Mana Flux Power Orb is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Power Orbs|Power Orb]] unlocked in [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer LVL VI (6)]]. It is an upgraded form of the [[Radiant Power Orb]], and it can be upgraded into an [[Overflux Power Orb]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wolf Slayer LVL VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Radiant Power Orb]] 4x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] 32x [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] ×4 + [[Golden Tooth]] x8 ×4 + [[Radiant Power Orb]] → Mana Flux Power Orb|
## Usage
Deployable:
Mana Flux Power Orb
[Effects]
[Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK; Place an orb for 1m buffing up to 5 players within 18 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max; Vitality Cost: 50 Vitality; Orb Buff: Mana Flux; Heal yourself for 40 Health per second.; Heal others for 20 Health per second.; Gain +50 Health Regen.; Grants +50% base mana regen.; Grants +10 Strength.; Only one deployable buff applies.]
Using its ability does not remove or use up the item. When placed, it cannot be picked up; the ability spawns a new orb.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Overflux Power Orb]]|Wolf Slayer LVL VII (7) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Overflux Capacitor]] [[Mana Flux Power Orb]] 1x Mana Flux Power Orb 24x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] x32 ×4 + [[Overflux Capacitor]] + [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] x8 ×3 + Mana Flux Power Orb → [[Overflux Power Orb]]|
## Tips
- If the player is planning on doing a decent amount of Slayers or the Campfire Trials quest, this item is essential and should be obtained as soon as possible.
## Trivia
- While [[Overflux Power Orb]], [[Overflux Capacitor]] and [[Plasmaflux Power Orb]] are all spelled as one word, this deployable splits Mana Flux into two words.
